# Buttons & Locators — FTTH List (Objekte)

## Summary

| Metric | Count |
|--------|-------|
| Total interactive elements | 4770 |
| Elements with stable locator | 4 |
| Elements needing stable locator | 4766 |

> A "stable locator" means the element has at least one of: `data-testid`, `aria-label`, or `id`.
> **99.9%** of elements currently lack a stable locator — this is primarily driven by 4624 `div[role="menuitem"]` dropdown options and ~50 icon-only row action buttons.

### Priority Actions

1. **Row action buttons (50 instances)** — add `aria-label="Open row details"` and `data-testid="d2d-ftth-row-action"` (or index-specific variant)
2. **Icon-only buttons (11 instances)** — add descriptive `aria-label` values and `data-testid` per control
3. **Dropdown options (4624 instances)** — wrap in `role="menu"` container and add `aria-label` per option value
4. **Table rows (25 instances)** — add `data-testid="d2d-ftth-row-{index}"` or scope by visible cell content
5. **Search button** — add `aria-label="Suchen"` and `data-testid="d2d-objects-search-button"`
